IMU-SSL · controlled multi-task benchmark

IMU-Bench Board

Wrist-IMU encoders adapted to six tasks under one recipe per task, one scorer per task, one result schema. Every cell is a JSON in bench/results/; nothing is typed by hand. Snapshot 2026-09-25T08:09:46-05:00 · git unknown.

Frozen probe

Encoder frozen, task head trained. Best per column tinted; #n = rank within task; † = recipe-equivalent legacy run; ‡ = uses the promoted ~12-layer probe decoder while every other encoder in that column still uses the 1-layer default — not like-for-like, and the rank next to it is therefore not a fair rank.

Probe-decoder capacity — mae_4src_z

Same frozen encoder, same data and recipe; only the task head changes, from the board's 1-layer default to ~12-layer decoders. Not comparable to the frozen-probe table above — these cells are an ablation and are excluded from the ranking until one decoder is promoted into the task cards for every encoder. Best per column tinted across the mae_4src_z rows only.

Probe decoderArm posepos err (cm) ↓Full-body poseMPJPE (mm) ↓OdometryATE (m) ↓
small decoder board default — 1× layer8.1498.09·
12 layers — balanced
pose: 4 cross-channel + 8 temporal, 256-d/8h · odom: 12×256-d/8h		8.0591.93·
12 layers — temporal-heavy
pose: 2 cross-channel + 10 temporal, 256-d/8h · odom: 12×192-d/4h		8.1392.33·
12 layers — wide
pose: 4 cross-channel + 8 temporal, 384-d/6h · odom: 12×384-d/6h		8.0591.64·
Scratch (no pretraining) same encoder architecture, random init, fully trained8.2192.720.1618
MobilePoser (Xu et al.) expert model, its own recipe9.7491.88·
TLIO expert model, its own recipe··0.1874
Air-IO expert model, its own recipe··0.1803

Contamination caveat. Every cell in the 3×3 grid above ran with a concurrent duplicate job writing into the same run directory for part of training (a dual-partition submission bug, fixed 2026-09-04). Single-job re-runs of the promoted arms reproduce full-body (91.64 → 91.45 mm) and odometry (0.1224 → 0.1228 ATE) but NOT arm pose: the promoted d12w decoder scores 8.14–8.16 cm when run alone, i.e. no better than the 1-layer default. Treat the arm-pose row as noise, not a win. The `mae_4src_z` numbers in the frozen-probe table are from the clean single-job re-runs.

Task cards — unified input / output

Arm pose

Nymeria real right-wrist IMU → 5-joint right-arm chain rotations
Data / split
Nymeria (real wrist IMU + Project-Aria mocap)
fixed pre-partitioned session directories (session-disjoint)
Input
[6, 512] 10.24 s @ 50 Hz, FLU, gravity included
Output
[P_t=32, J=5, 6] — 6D-continuous local rotations of SMPL joints [14,17,19,21,23] (collar, shoulder, elbow, wrist, hand), one per 0.32 s patch
Head
ViT1DPoseDecoder (2 layers, hidden 192)
Selection
val_loss (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
pos err (cm) ↓
FK joint-position error at the 5 arm joints with GT ancestors (scripts/eval_arm_pose_position_error.py)
Scorer
bench.score.arm_pose

Full-body pose

Nymeria real right-wrist IMU → 23-joint SMPL body (root excluded)
Data / split
Nymeria (real wrist IMU + Project-Aria mocap)
fixed pre-partitioned session directories (session-disjoint)
Input
[6, 512] 10.24 s @ 50 Hz, FLU, gravity included
Output
[P_t=32, J=23, 6] — 6D local rotations for SMPL joints 1..23, one per 0.32 s patch (sub_patches=1)
Head
ViT1DPoseDecoder
Selection
mpjpe_mm (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
MPJPE (mm) ↓
root-relative FK joint position error, joints 1-23 (imu_lm/objectives/pose_1d.py::_compute_val_mpjpe), at the selected checkpoint
Scorer
bench.score.full_body_pose

Odometry

Nymeria real right-wrist IMU → pelvis velocity in the wrist (FLU) frame
Data / split
Nymeria (real wrist IMU + Project-Aria pelvis trajectory)
fixed pre-partitioned session directories (session-disjoint)
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, FLU, gravity included
Output
[P_t=8, K=4, 3] — 3-D pelvis velocity (m/s) expressed in the wrist FLU frame at each sub-patch midpoint (imu_lm/data/nymeria_real_wrist_odom_loader_FLU.py), 4 sub-patches per 0.32 s patch (0.08 s resolution)
Head
ViT1DOdomDecoder (1 layer, hidden 192; 12 layers for the frozen probe)
Selection
val_loss (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
ATE (m) ↓
mean absolute trajectory error over all val windows; predicted FLU velocities are rotated to the world frame with the ground-truth start orientation propagated by raw-gyro integration, then summed (bench/score/odometry_local.py, = scripts/eval_odom_nymeria_altattn.py::reconstruct_and_score)
Scorer
bench.score.odometry_local

Microgesture

OpenWatch wrist IMU → 5 gestures + background (paper protocol, real recordings)
Data / split
OpenWatch (Bonazzi et al.), open_watch_without_augmentations
contamination-safe posture-stratified subject split over canonical identities, seed 42, 80/10/10 (33/5/5 subjects; val 102 clips / test 87 clips)
Input
[6, 128] 1.0 s @ 100 Hz, FLU, gravity included
Output
[6] — class logits per 1 s window; clip label = first run of k=3 identical window predictions, else majority (imu_lm/utils/clip_voting.py)
Head
LinearHead(384, 6)
Selection
macro_f1 (max) on val
Reported
test split
Primary
clip F1 (test) ↑
Scorer
bench.score.microgesture

Language alignment

HumanML3D synthetic wrist IMU (UniMTS_data) ↔ text retrieval
Data / split
HumanML3D / UniMTS_data sim (right-wrist channel), aug_texts captions
HumanML3D official train/val/test lists (val 1,197 clips; test 3,701 clips)
Input
[6, 512] 10.24 s @ 50 Hz, FLU, gravity included
Output
[512] — L2-normalised IMU embedding in CLIP ViT-B/32 text space (ContrastiveCLIPHead); text tower trainable, i2t InfoNCE, τ=0.1 fixed
Head
ContrastiveCLIPHead(384→512) + CLIP text tower
Selection
fixed_budget (latest) on none
Reported
test split
Primary
R@1 G=100 (mean i2t/t2i) ↑
mean of IMU→Text and Text→IMU Recall@1 over 200 random 100-item galleries, first-caption protocol
Scorer
bench.score.language

Parkinson's disease

PADS smartwatch IMU → {Healthy, PD, Differential diagnosis} per subject
Data / split
PADS (PhysioNet 1.0.0), 469 patients, 11 tasks × 2 wrists
subject-disjoint 80/20 (375 / 94 subjects), splits.group_key=subject_id, seed 0
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, FLU, gravity NOT included (Apple CoreMotion userAcceleration)
Output
[3] — class logits per 2.56 s window; subject prediction = argmax of mean softmax over all of the subject's windows
Head
LinearHead(384, 3)
Selection
bal_acc (max) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
subject macro-F1 ↑
mean-prob aggregation over ~180 windows/subject (scripts/eval_pads_subject_level.py), macro-F1 over HC/PD/DD; a class never predicted contributes F1=0. Chance ≈ 0.25-0.33
Scorer
bench.score.pd

HAR · HHAR

HHAR activity recognition from one wrist/arm IMU (6 classes)
Data / split
HHAR: smartwatch acc+gyro (Stisen et al. 2015), 6 activities, 9 users x 4 watches; asof-aligned, 50 Hz canonical
subject-disjoint 20 % val split (splits.val_ratio 0.2, seed 42); val is reported (no test split in v1)
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, sensor, gravity included
Output
[K=6] — class logits over the 6 activity labels, one per 2.56 s window
Head
LinearHead on mean-pooled tokens
Selection
val_loss (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
macro-F1 ↑
window-level macro-F1 on the val split at the min-val-loss checkpoint (trainer eval, imu_lm/objectives/supervised.py)
Scorer
bench.score.har

subject-disjoint 20 % val split (splits.val_ratio 0.2, seed 42); val is reported (no test split in v1)
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, sensor, gravity included
Output
[K=12] — class logits over the 12 activity labels, one per 2.56 s window
Head

val split

macro-F1 ↑

HAR · User-study watch

User-study smartwatch, 60 fine-grained everyday activities (10 participants)
Data / split
User-study watch (ours): 10 participants x 7 activities each, 70 recordings, 2.0 h, 60 activity classes after merging spelling variants; wrist smartwatch acc (user accel + gravity, m/s^2) + gyro (rad/s), 50 Hz
TIME split within each recording: first 80 % train, last 20 % val, one 2.56 s window dropped at the boundary (splits.split_by_session_suffix). Participants appear in both splits -- NOT subject-disjoint; most activities were performed by a single participant
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, sensor, gravity included
Output
[K=60] — class logits over the 60 activity labels, one per 2.56 s window
Head
LinearHead on mean-pooled tokens
Selection
val_loss (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
macro-F1 ↑
window-level macro-F1 on the val split at the min-val-loss checkpoint (trainer eval, imu_lm/objectives/supervised.py)
Scorer
bench.score.har

HAR · PRISM

PrISM-Tracker (Arakawa et al.) — PRISM activity recognition from one wrist IMU (5 global ADL classes, 31 subjects)
Data / split
PRISM: held-out probe dataset (base.yaml splits.probe_dataset), 31 subjects, never in any pretraining pool; 16.3 h, 20 sessions, 96 dataset_activity_id labels (head size = the trainer's own label map)
subject-disjoint 20 % val split (splits.val_ratio 0.2, seed 42); val is reported
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, sensor, gravity included
Output
[K] — class logits over the dataset's activity labels, one per 2.56 s window
Head
LinearHead on mean-pooled tokens
Selection
val_loss (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
macro-F1 ↑
window-level macro-F1 on the val split at the min-val-loss checkpoint, re-evaluated with pooled predictions (bench.score.har)
Scorer
bench.score.har

Fall detection · SmartFallMM

SmartFallMM right-wrist IMU → fall vs activity of daily living (31 participants)
Data / split
SmartFallMM (Texas State SmartFall group): right-wrist MbientLab Meta sensor, acc + gyro, 50 Hz; 31 young participants, 1,352 trials (538 simulated falls onto an air mattress in 5 directions, 814 ADLs of 9 kinds), 3.6 h. Sensor ranges +-2 g / +-125 deg/s: the gyroscope saturates for a median 14 % of a trial's samples, the accelerometer at fall impacts
subject-disjoint 20 % val split (splits.val_ratio 0.2, seed 42); val is reported
Input
[6, 128] 2.56 s @ 50 Hz, sensor, gravity included
Output
[K=2] — fall / ADL logits, one per 2.56 s window (every window of a trial carries the trial's label)
Head
LinearHead on mean-pooled tokens
Selection
val_loss (min) on val
Reported
val split
Primary
macro-F1 ↑
window-level macro-F1 on the val split at the min-val-loss checkpoint (trainer eval, imu_lm/objectives/supervised.py)
Scorer
bench.score.har

Caveats that travel with the numbers

  • Pose, odometry and PD select and report on val (no test split exists for the fixed Nymeria/PADS partitions in v1); microgesture and language report a genuine test split.
  • Full-body MPJPE is best-of-curve on val — noise-mined by ±1–2 mm; multi-seed cells are what to trust.
  • Language: fixed 8k-step budget, final checkpoint; seed sd ≈ 0.7 MRR.
  • PADS subject-level balanced accuracy is over 94 val subjects (14 HC / 58 PD / 22 DD).
  • External rows come from their own scripts and are context, not competitors under the encoder ranking.
Regenerate: python -m bench.leaderboard && python -m bench.leaderboard_html. Registry: bench/registry/*.yaml.
